Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Theorem 217.1000 (Existence and Uniqueness)

Statement: For any quantum_chemistry module M satisfying the existence and uniqueness hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 717. The projective resolution has length at most 35 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We apply the existence and uniqueness principle to the quantum_chemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^717 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 35-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 217.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The quantum_chemistry variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 484 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 762 and intersection form of rank 2.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
